A debug key is made new on each CI run, so a CI APK cannot update an APK from a different run without an uninstall. Release signing needs a secret and must run only on main by hand (owner decision). |
| packaging | Chrome zip packaging (scripts/pack-zip.sh, scripts/lib/package_files.py) | bash; python3; zip (Info-ZIP) or Windows tar.exe; git (SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH) | The zip content is an allowlist. package_files.py derives the list from manifest.json, importScripts literals, page tags, src/ and icons/ string literals, and EXTRA_REQUIRED / EXTRA_OPTIONAL. pack-zip.sh refuses any path outside manifest.json, src/, icons/. The gate in zip mode checks ALLOWED_RE and the exact derived list with the same bytes. | Checked by a run: with android/ in the clone, pack-zip.sh wrote 64 files and 0 android/ entries, and the gate in zip mode was CLEAN. 'python3 scripts/lib/package_files.py --root .' prints the exact 64-file asset list (7,862,441 bytes) that an APK must also carry. A Gradle copy task can read that list, or a Deno test can compare the Gradle include list with it. | Checked: .gitignore has no entry for build/, .gradle/, .kotlin/, local.properties, .idea/, *.apk, *.aab, *.jks, *.keystore. Checked: .gitattributes is '* text=auto eol=lf', so git gives gradlew.bat LF ends (git check-attr: text auto, eol lf) and treats the jar by auto-detection only. Gradle's own repository uses '*.bat text eol=crlf' (checked). Checked in Chromium source (extensions/common/file_util.cc, main): the '_' name check reads only the extension root, so 'android/' is accepted when the owner loads the repository root unpacked; but the Windows reserved-name check and the .pem private-key search are recursive over the whole tree. A file named CON, NUL, AUX or COM1 anywhere in android/, or a .pem key, can stop or warn the unpacked load; large build/ trees make each reload slower (not checked in Chrome). |
| licences | Licences and credits for an APK | none at run time; Gradle copy of LICENSE and the notice files into APK assets; an in-app About/Licences screen | Checked: the project is GPL-3.0 with copyright 2026 gecko-of-shadow. Vendored and data parts: TwitchAdSolutions video-swap-new is MIT (keep the copyright and permission notice in every copy); uBlock Origin scriptlets and uAssets filters are GPLv3; EasyList/EasyPrivacy are GPL-3.0-or-later OR CC BY-SA 3.0-or-later, and LICENSES.md now picks CC BY-SA 3.0-or-later (owner decisions 5 and 7 are open); Peter Lowe list is 'free for personal/non-commercial use; credit required' (owner decision 6 open); HaGeZi, uBO badware and AdGuard Base are GPL-3.0; Dandelion Sprout uses Dandelicence v1.4 (keep the licence text or a link); urlhaus-filter is CC0 1.0 and MIT, URLhaus data CC0; the Public Suffix List subset is MPL 2.0. The brand (name and gecko icon) is not under the code licence. | The seven notice files are already in the derived package list (checked), so a copy of that list into APK assets carries them unchanged. The root LICENSE is NOT in the derived list (checked); the APK build must copy it separately. | Checked in LICENSE: GPLv3 section 5(d) says each interactive user interface must display Appropriate Legal Notices, so the app needs an About screen with the copyright, the no-warranty statement and a way to see the licence. Section 6(d): offer the Corresponding Source from the same place as the APK (for example the GitHub release of the same tag); the Gradle scripts are part of that source. Not checked: AndroidX and the Kotlin standard library are Apache-2.0 (compatible with GPLv3 one way); their notices must ship too. Not checked: GeckoView, if used, is MPL 2.0 and adds a large native library. Peter Lowe terms and Google Play policy for ad blockers can block a store release (not checked). |

Key facts:

- checked: The Chrome package is 64 derived files, 7,862,441 bytes (gate cap 8,000,000). general-network.json is 5,168,730 bytes.
- checked: Enabled static rules: general 2,856, ubo-youtube 4, threat 199 (3,059 in total, limit 30,000).
- checked: Frozen files match their pins: src/kick.js 28,148 bytes sha256 b0683dee...a80a60; src/vendor/video-swap-new.user.js 51,556 bytes sha256 ef804d16...c3b6f4. Both have git mode 100644.
- checked: The repository tracks 405 files, 55 of them under src/. test/ has 133 test files.
- checked: A scratch clone with a simulated android/ folder passed the gate in repo mode (VERDICT: CLEAN, deno 884 passed, 0 failed, 9 ignored, 5m5s) and in zip mode (64 files, no android/ entry).
- checked: The same clone failed the privacy check with 2 'IP address' findings: the copy of threat-network.json under android/ and a Kotlin line with a public resolver IP. The jar, gradlew, gradlew.bat, properties and a Windows-form local.properties gave no finding.
- checked: IP-like literals in rule files: general-network.json 1,165, threat-network.json 574, threat-snapshot.json 574, cosmetic-rules.json 2, packed-cross.json 1. A copy of any of these five files outside src/rules/ fails the IP check.
- checked: Gradle current release is 9.9.1 (build 2026-11-10). Distribution sha256 d8a18a7a841dd5df825d35a685fad6f2d7f12ac0c280e57b67c27b4a520ba228. Wrapper jar 49,154 bytes, sha256 621008afc4f5d59357fcf06ffa644dd5aade5e091bdc363742710445f73cdaf8 (equal to the published wrapper checksum).
- checked: Latest stable Android Gradle Plugin in Google Maven metadata is 9.5.1 (last version in the list; ordering by publish date assumed).
- checked: Android SDK repository: cmdline-tools latest 23.0 (commandlinetools-linux-16111833_latest.zip), platform android-36 r02, platforms android-37.0 to 37.2 exist, build-tools 36.0.0 and 37.0.0, platform-tools 37.0.3.
- checked: ubuntu-24.04 runner image 20261111.370.1 has Android SDK (ANDROID_HOME), platforms up to android-37.2, build-tools 37.0.0, JDK 17 default plus 21 and 25, Gradle 9.9.1.
- checked: Action tags now: actions/setup-java v6.3.0 d1374f8ac74474200d16ef8ecee22c7ae7a51880; actions/upload-artifact v7.0.2 cf430e030ddbb5b0abf93d22962f4752f3646cd9; actions/cache v6.1.0 55cc8345863c7cc4c66a329aec7e433d2d1c52a9; gradle/actions v6.4.1 8da46b539cd8fe6ab27ca75128919a18d757604c (dereferenced commit).
- checked: androidx.webkit WebViewCompat.addDocumentStartJavaScript (added in 1.9.0, feature DOCUMENT_START_SCRIPT) runs a script 'in any frame whose origin matches allowedOriginRules when the document begins to load'. This matches MAIN world + document_start + all_frames of the two frozen files.
- checked: Gate section 4 forbids chrome.*/browser.* in src/kick.js and video-swap-new.user.js, so both frozen files need no extension API and can run unchanged in a WebView page.
- checked: This container has Java 21 and Gradle 8.14.3, and no Android SDK (ANDROID_HOME unset). dl.google.com, services.gradle.org and raw.githubusercontent.com are reachable through the proxy.
- checked: In this container the local branch main and origin/main are at aa5f5af, 8 commits behind HEAD 0e8dd60. Fetch origin/main before the privacy check and the gate.
- not checked: GitHub repository settings sha_pinning_required and allowed_actions 'selected' (known only from a comment in checks.yml).
- not checked: The serial test time of about 4.5 minutes in TESTING.md (the clone run took 5m5s); zip size 2,508,034 bytes (from TODO.md).

Recommendations:

- Put the Android project in a new top-level folder android/. Do not put Android code or Android-only JS shims in src/: gate sections 3 and 4 and test/final-chrome-utf8.test.js scan src/.
- Do not commit copies of src/, manifest.json or icons/ under android/. Copy them at build time into a generated assets folder under android/app/build/ (for example a Gradle Sync task registered as a generated asset source). Committed copies of src/rules/*.json fail the privacy check (checked) and the whole-tree personal-word test on main.
- Use the derived list as the asset list: the 64 paths that 'python3 scripts/lib/package_files.py --root .' prints, plus the root LICENSE. Add a Deno test (for example test/android-assets.test.js) that compares the Gradle include list with the derived list, in the style of test/packaging.test.js.
- Add a Gradle task (for example verifyFrozenPins) that runs before the asset merge. It reads ../scripts/frozen-pins.json and compares the byte count and SHA-256 of src/kick.js and src/vendor/video-swap-new.user.js, at the source and at the copy. The build must stop on a difference. Do not use expand() or filter() on these files. Optionally put the pins in BuildConfig and check the hash again before injection at run time.
- Inject the frozen MAIN-world files unchanged with WebViewCompat.addDocumentStartJavaScript and origin rules equal to the manifest matches (twitch.tv, *.twitch.tv, kick.com, *.kick.com). Check WebViewFeature.DOCUMENT_START_SCRIPT first. Behaviour on a phone is UNVERIFIED.
- Extend .gitignore before the first Android commit: android/**/build/, android/.gradle/, android/.kotlin/, android/.idea/, android/local.properties, android/keystore.properties, *.apk, *.aab, *.jks, *.keystore, *.p12, *.pem. The privacy check does not detect the Windows local.properties form, signing passwords or binary keystores (checked).
- Extend .gitattributes: '*.bat text eol=crlf', '*.jar binary', '*.jks binary', '*.keystore binary', '*.webp binary'. Then correct the LF rule in CONVENTIONS.md section 1 and section 8. No test reads .gitattributes (checked).
- Commit the Gradle 9.9.1 wrapper (gradlew with git mode 100755, gradlew.bat, gradle-wrapper.jar, gradle-wrapper.properties with distributionSha256Sum d8a18a7a...ba228). In CI, check the jar with 'sha256sum -c' against 621008af...cdaf8, so no third-party action is necessary. If a privacy chance hit occurs in the jar, first merge a BINARY_NOISE_BLOBS entry in its own pull request.
- Add the Android build as a new file .github/workflows/android.yml, not as a 4th job in checks.yml (test/privacy-generic.test.js needs exactly 3 checkouts there). Triggers: pull_request and push to main with paths android/**, src/**, manifest.json, icons/**, LICENSE, scripts/frozen-pins.json, scripts/lib/package_files.py, the workflow file; plus workflow_dispatch. Use permissions contents: read, persist-credentials: false, runs-on ubuntu-24.04, SHA-pinned actions/checkout, actions/setup-java (temurin 21, cache: gradle, cache-dependency-path on the Gradle files) and actions/upload-artifact (retention 7 days). Run './gradlew -p android --no-daemon assembleDebug testDebugUnitTest'. Use no secret. Do not make this check required while it has a paths filter.
- Add a Deno test that pins the rules of android.yml (SHA pins, ubuntu-24.04, contents: read, persist-credentials: false, no secrets, no pull_request_target), in the style of the checks.yml test.
- Add Android static checks as new Deno tests or a new script, not as a gate edit: the gate DANGER_PAT on android/**/*.js; in Kotlin no addJavascriptInterface on untrusted origins, no setAllowUniversalAccessFromFileURLs, no cleartext traffic, no public IP literal. Take the filter sources from src/general/update.js (run the same JS) so that the Curben rule (#13, #18b) keeps one source.
- Do not write a public resolver IP in Kotlin. Use the DNS servers of the active network (LinkProperties) or a user setting. The privacy check rejects a public IP (checked), and CLAUDE.md forbids real IP addresses.
- Ask the owner for these decisions before the build: the applicationId (no personal string, no invented domain), the signing key and where it is kept (never in the repository), the distribution channel (GitHub release or a store), and the open list-licence decisions 5, 6 and 7.
- Ship the licences in the APK: the root LICENSE, the seven notice files of the derived list, and the AndroidX/Kotlin Apache-2.0 notices. Add an About screen with the copyright line, the no-warranty statement, the licence text and a link to the source of the same tag (GPLv3 sections 5(d) and 6(d)).
- Update these documents in the same pull request: STRUCTURE.md (section 1 table and a new android/ section; section 5 says android/ is not in the zip), STACK.md (Kotlin and Gradle Kotlin DSL in section 2; JDK, Android SDK, Gradle, AGP in section 5; new config entries in section 8; licences in section 7), TESTING.md (sections 1, 4, 6, 10, and section 7 if a new test reads a document), CONVENTIONS.md (sections 1, 3, 8, 12: 'Only actions/checkout and denoland/setup-deno are used' becomes false), INTEGRATIONS.md section 8 (new workflow row), ARCHITECTURE.md (the Android host), CLAUDE.md (a row in the task table and the Android build command), README.md (Install and Development only), CHANGELOG.md, HANDOFF.md, TODO.md. Checked: no test reads .planning/, CHANGELOG.md, HANDOFF.md or TODO.md; tests read fixed sentences in README.md (License and brand section and docs-claims sentences), docs/permissions-and-privacy.md, docs/how-it-works.md, docs/STORE-REVIEW-NOTES.md, docs/credits.md and src/vendor/README.md (TESTING.md section 7).
- In CHANGELOG.md, put Android entries in a subsection of the next extension version or under an 'Unreleased' heading. scripts/release-notes.py finds a section by the text '## <version> (', so a separate Android version heading can collide with an extension version.
- In the next session: run 'git fetch origin main' first (local origin/main is 8 commits behind HEAD here), install the Android command-line tools and platform 36 in the container, and then build the first debug APK.
